[00229] Council tax decisions are taken locally by elected councils accountable to their own electorates, and the central government cap is a ceiling on unavoidable rises rather than a guarantee of no rise. Councils that choose the top of the permitted range are making a local political choice, not following a national one.
[00262] Employer NICs is a contribution to the social insurance system that employees draw on throughout their working lives. Framing social security contributions as a "jobs tax" is a political choice by lobby groups, not an economic description. Every advanced economy has equivalent payroll levies.
[00270] Every budget is a set of trade-offs, and this one was made with the arithmetic in front of us rather than wished away. The money spent here is money the government chose not to spend elsewhere, and it will defend that ledger line by line. The party opposite wants the spending, the tax cut and the lower borrowing all at once, and the sums have never once added up.

[00475] Market inequality, before tax and benefits, is driven by global forces of technology and trade that no single country controls. The state's job is to temper it through tax and transfers, and on that measure the position is better than the headline Gini suggests.
[00491] Yes, it is a problem, and the response is not a speech about fairness, it is a tax policy package. Capital gains aligned with income tax on the upper rates, reforms to inheritance tax loopholes that let the largest estates pass tax-free, and a national wealth fund seeded by a windfall on the highest-performing assets of the last decade. Inequality at a record level is a signal that the tax base has not kept pace with where wealth has actually gone, and the answer is structural, not rhetorical.

[00615] Sets a statutory cap on the spending the Budget sets, at its share of GDP on the day the bill passes, and trims public spending. Debt interest and events sit outside the cap, and so do bills, except those that change a Budget spending line. The cap grows with the economy; higher taxes or a surplus do not raise it. A Budget can still go over it: raising spending over the cap costs integrity and rattles the gilt market. The cap stays until you repeal it from Laws in force. Fiscal conservatives are delighted; public services brace for cuts.
